./specs/system-deep-loop/loop-architecture/002-graph-engineering/007-expansive-depth-mode/014-docs-playbooks-alignment/scratch/skilled-alignment-run-3/lanes/B.raw.txt:1781:.skilled/commands/deep/review.md:97:| `--depth-mode` | `default` \| `expansive` | `default`, the value when the flag is absent, runs exactly as before. `expansive` is opt-in: the run seeds depth-1 sub-target nodes from the review target before iteration 1 (record the pilot range SHA; merges are pruned as `merge-no-own-diff` and index rebuilds as `generated-artifact`), admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps (`expansiveDepth` in the config: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths), keeps node status in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, holds open nodes as `searchDebt`, dispatches the active node's next uncovered dimension, and turns a review stop candidate into another iteration while nodes stay open. 97:| `--depth-mode` | `default` \| `expansive` | `default`, the value when the flag is absent, runs exactly as before. `expansive` is opt-in: the run seeds depth-1 sub-target nodes from the review target before iteration 1 (record the pilot range SHA; merges are pruned as `merge-no-own-diff` and index rebuilds as `generated-artifact`), admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps (`expansiveDepth` in the config: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths), keeps node status in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, holds open nodes as `searchDebt`, dispatches the active node's next uncovered dimension, and turns a review stop candidate into another iteration while nodes stay open. A node needs one iteration per dimension in its universe, the four canonical dimensions plus any custom name, and the depth-1 band gets only `budgetSplit.depth1` (0.5) of `--max-iterations`, so set `--max-iterations` to at least `2 * ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions` to fund the depth-1 band. It never outranks the iteration cap, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`. Fan-out lineages each get a share of the seeded nodes. |

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && rg -n "expansive|extend|minIterations|custom|frontier|depth-mode|DRV-07|DRV-08" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/feature-catalog/feature-catalog.md | head -80
184:### Completed-run extension (--extend)
188:A completed deep-review run extends in place by N iterations under --extend=N, keeps its session identifier and generation, and keeps the prior review report as a snapshot before re-synthesis.
192:The run continues under lineage mode `completed-continue`, keeps the prior review report as a snapshot before re-synthesis and takes `--extend-minutes=M` on the duration budget. Extension is refused with fan-out.
200:### Review minIterations floor
204:A review run with a configured minIterations floor returns CONTINUE with minIterationsNotReached until its iteration count reaches the floor, so a review never stops before the floor.
208:`convergence.cjs` returns `CONTINUE` with `minIterationsNotReached` below the floor, and the command's fixed stop order checks the floor before the gates. The shipped default is 2.
216:### Expansive depth mode (--depth-mode=expansive)
220:Opt-in --depth-mode=expansive on deep-review seeds depth-one sub-target nodes from the review target, keeps node status in review/frontier-nodes.jsonl, and holds a review stop while nodes stay open.
224:Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, open nodes count as `searchDebt`, and the divergent pivot waits for a terminal frontier. The live pilot runs are still open, so no pilot result is recorded here.
228:See [`loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md`](loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.
436:A review run accepts configured custom dimensions after the four canonical ones, tracks them in dimension coverage, and lets a covered custom dimension reach the all-dimensions-clean stop.
444:See [`review-dimensions/custom-review-dimensions.md`](review-dimensions/custom-review-dimensions.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.
540:`deep-review-config.json` declares `antiConvergence.minIterations = 2`, `convergenceMode = "default"`, and `stopPolicy = "fail-closed"`. The runtime capability matrix repeats the fail-closed policy, the shared resolver rejects missing or permissive policy, and the optimizer manifest locks convergence mode while rejecting `minIterations > maxIterations` candidates.

104:| `--depth-mode` | `default` \| `expansive` | `default`, the value when the flag is absent, runs exactly as before. `expansive` is opt-in: the run seeds depth-1 frontier nodes from the topic before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows as new questions under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps (`expansiveDepth` in the config: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths), steers Next Focus to the shallowest open node, and turns a convergence stop into another iteration while frontier nodes stay open. It never outranks the iteration cap, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`. Fan-out lineages each get a share of the seeded nodes. |
111:| `--extend` | positive integer, as `--extend=N` | Extends a completed run in place by `N` iterations, lineage mode `completed-continue`. The run keeps its `sessionId` and generation, records one `run_extended` ledger event and keeps the prior synthesis as `research/synthesis-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md`. The run must be complete, so use `--lineage-mode=resume` for an unfinished one. Refused with fan-out. Under `stopPolicy: convergence` an extension with nothing new can re-stop on `all_questions_answered` at once: pass `--convergence-mode=off`, use `stopPolicy: max-iterations` or add questions to `research/inbox.jsonl`. |
112:| `--extend-minutes` | positive integer, as `--extend-minutes=M` | Minutes added to the duration budget on an extension, default the prior `maxDurationMinutes`. The new budget is `max(prior, elapsed) + M`, because the clock counts idle time since `createdAt`, so an extension started after the old budget still runs. Valid only with `--extend`. |

commit 54042891bf5957a8f4e24ca3371c094442f0633c
Author: Michel Kerkmeester <redacted>
Date:   Wed Nov 18 01:12:31 2026 +0100

    fix(system-deep-loop): keep answered frontier nodes out of the budget close
    
    The research reducer applied the depth-band budget to the raw node store
    before the answered projection ran. Every store row stays open, so an
    overspent band closed answered nodes as exhausted, and an answered band never
    released its unspent share to the next band. The pilot showed 10 answered
    nodes turn into 4 answered and 8 exhausted after one more iteration. The
    budget now runs inside the registry build, after the iteration evidence marks
    answered nodes, and closes only nodes still open. A budget-closed node still
    resolves its question.
    
    Commit-Id: 0014837

commit f0f08e96f3db643fe6b72815a8fb520120b19f0f
Author: Michel Kerkmeester <redacted>
Date:   Wed Nov 18 01:16:35 2026 +0100

    fix(system-deep-loop): reduce once after the frontier seed
    
    Under the expansive depth mode the loop renders the frontier block from the
    strategy's next focus, and only the reducer writes that focus. Nothing reduced
    between the seed and the first iteration, so iteration 1 ran on the template
    focus with no frontier node and no frontier block. A single expansive-gated
    init step now runs the reducer after the strategy exists, and a lifecycle
    test runs it on a seeded packet and renders the block from the focus it wrote.
    
    Commit-Id: 0014838

commit 9e0b02c49a6178776643ea85c9d1c9aada99d3f4
Author: Michel Kerkmeester <redacted>
Date:   Wed Nov 18 01:18:47 2026 +0100

    fix(system-deep-loop): repair expansive review frontier defects from the pilot
    
    A live expansive review pilot over a 400-commit range found four defects on
    the frontier path. The root shared the per-node children cap, so a review seed
    admitted six depth-1 sub-targets although the workflow promises three to eight;
    the root cap is now the top of each loop's documented seed range (eight for
    review, six for research) and never below the per-node cap, which stays as it
    was. A commit-range target typed as a file list made the range string the only
    allowed path, so every depth-2 proposal pruned as irrelevant; relevance now
    intersects a range target with the files scope discovery recorded for it.
    Merged frontier nodes kept lineage-local ids, so three lineages each reported
    F-2-002; merged ids and parent links are now lineage-qualified, with the local
    id kept beside them for the partition guard. The review docs now name the
    frontier_partition key the runner records in orchestration-summary.json.
    
    Every change sits on the expansive path; both depth-mode golden suites pass.
    
    Commit-Id: 0014839

+// A git revision range (base..tip or base...tip) is not a path. The dots must sit
+// between two revision characters, so a '../' path segment never reads as one.
+function isCommitRange(value) {
+  return typeof value === 'string' && /[^\s/.]\.\.\.?[^\s/.]/.test(value) && splitTargetPaths(value).length === 1;
+}
+
+// A commit range names no paths itself; the files it touches are its scope, and
+// scope discovery records them. A proposal is relevant when every entry is one of
+// those files or a directory holding one.
+function scopeInsideTouchedFiles(scope, touchedFiles) {
+  const files = (Array.isArray(touchedFiles) ? touchedFiles : splitTargetPaths(touchedFiles))
+    .filter((entry) => typeof entry === 'string')
+    .map(normalizeTargetPath)
+    .filter((entry) => entry !== '');
+  if (files.length === 0) return true;
+

+  )));

# Deep Start Review Loop

Thin router for the deep-review loop. This command verifies the orchestrating agent, resolves setup and execution mode, loads the presentation contract, then executes the owned workflow YAML.

### MANDATORY INPUT GATE

- **DO NOT** dispatch any agent from this document.
- **ALL** agent dispatching is handled by the YAML workflow steps.
- **MARKDOWN OWNS SETUP**: resolve setup inputs here first, then hand off to YAML.
- **YAML START CONDITION**: do not load YAML until ALL required inputs are bound:
  - `review_target`, `review_target_type`, `review_dimensions`, `spec_folder`, `execution_mode`, `maxIterations`, `convergenceThreshold`, `convergence_mode`, `depth_mode`, `stop_policy`, `lineage_mode`
  - `dry_run` (default false unless `--dry-run` is present)

## 1. ROUTER CONTRACT
==== research ====

export type DeepResearchEventStem = typeof DeepResearchEventStems[number];

// A registration is only honest when every stem either has a mechanical
// producer or is explicitly reserved. `spoken` names the files that emit the
// stem; `reserved` records why nothing emits it yet and what would earn a
// producer. The stem-producer checker holds this table to the emitter surface
// on disk and fails when the two disagree.
export type DeepResearchStemProducerStatus =
  | { readonly status: 'spoken'; readonly producers: readonly string[] }
  | { readonly status: 'reserved'; readonly reason: string };

if (require.main === module) {
  let status;
  try {
    status = main();
  } catch (error) {
    fail(error);
  }
  process.exit(status);

function emit(payload) {
  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(payload)}\n`);
}

function fail(error) {
  emit({ error: error && error.message ? error.message : String(error) });
  process.exit(1);
}

function parseArgs(argv) {
  const out = { repoRoot: null };
> build · deepseek-v4.1-flash
[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '380,422p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/check-ledger-stem-producers.cjs
process.stdout.write(
      'Usage: check-ledger-stem-producers.cjs [--repo-root <path>]\n'
      + 'Exit 0 when every registered stem is declared and every spoken stem has an emitter.\n',
    );
    return 0;
  }
  if (args.error) {
    process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ error: args.error })}\n`);
    return 1;
  }

  let repoRoot;
  try {
    repoRoot = args.repoRoot ? path.resolve(args.repoRoot) : defaultRepoRoot();
  } catch (error) {
    process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ error: error.message })}\n`);
    return 1;
  }

  const { emitters, interpolated, missingFiles } = scanEmitters(repoRoot);
  const results = MODES.map((spec) => evaluateMode(spec, repoRoot, emitters, missingFiles, interpolated));
  const violations = results
    .flatMap((result) => result.violations.map((violation) => ({ mode: result.mode, ...violation })))
    .sort((left, right) => {
      const leftKey = `${left.mode}|${left.stem ?? ''}|${left.file ?? ''}|${left.rule}`;
      const rightKey = `${right.mode}|${right.stem ?? ''}|${right.file ?? ''}|${right.rule}`;
      return leftKey < rightKey ? -1 : leftKey > rightKey ? 1 : 0;
    });
  const ok = violations.length === 0;

  emit({
    ok,
    repoRoot,
    registered: results.reduce((total, result) => total + result.registered, 0),
    spoken: results.reduce((total, result) => total + result.spoken, 0),
    reserved: results.reduce((total, result) => total + result.reserved, 0),
    emitters: results.flatMap((result) => result.emitters),
    violations,
  });
  return ok ? 0 : 2;
}
